Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the satellites framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 972. The resulting operator achieves bound O(863/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 972 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the satellites structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 863. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
